- **accidental-data-loss-prevention**: **STOP AND VERIFY**: Before running any command or tool that results in irreversible data loss, you MUST obtain explicit user consent. When in doubt, ask. It is better to wait for confirmation than to accidentally delete production data or critical project assets. Use this for: - SQL: DROP TABLE/VIEW/SCHEMA/DATABASE, TRUNCATE, or broad DELETE (missing WHERE or using 1=1). - Cloud Storage: gsutil rm or gcloud storage rm targeting production data or critical buckets. - Infrastructure: gcloud projects delete, deleting Spanner/BigQuery/Dataproc resources, deleting secrets, or KMS key destruction. ([GoogleCloudPlatform/data-agent-kit-plugin](https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/data-agent-kit-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/accidental-data-loss-prevention) · ★252 · 66 copies)
- **accidental-data-loss-prevention**: **DETENTE Y VERIFICA**: Antes de ejecutar cualquier comando o herramienta que produzca una pérdida de datos irreversible, DEBES obtener el consentimiento explícito del usuario. Ante la duda, pregunta. Es mejor esperar la confirmación que eliminar por accidente datos de producción o recursos críticos del proyecto. Úsala para: - SQL: DROP TABLE/VIEW/SCHEMA/DATABASE, TRUNCATE, o DELETE masivo (sin WHERE o usando 1=1). - Cloud Storage: gsutil rm o gcloud storage rm dirigidos a datos de producción o buckets críticos. - Infraestructura: gcloud projects delete, eliminación de recursos de Spanner/Big… ([sebastian-gsz/agent-arsenal](https://github.com/sebastian-gsz/agent-arsenal/tree/HEAD/google-secure-coder/modules/securecoder/accidental-data-loss-prevention) · ★1)
- **accidental-data-loss-prevention**: Gate for irreversible destructive actions (DROP/TRUNCATE, broad DELETE, volume prune/reset, rm -rf data): audit isolation first and get explicit user consent before running.
